) for the odd cases.

B.8.
Proof of Proposition
5.1

Note that for
i\geq 1
,
m_{i}
recursively derives the numbers
m_{i+1-j}^{(j)}
and the pairs
(\lambda_{2j-1}^{(i+1-j)},\lambda_{2j}^{(i+1-j)})
for
1\leq j\leq i
, as the
b^{(k,l)}_{i-j}
inserted into the pair
(\lambda_{2j+1},\lambda_{2j+2})
can only be provided by the
b^{(k,l)}_{i+1-j}
inserted into the pair
(\lambda_{2j-1},\lambda_{2j})
.
For
n
the greatest index
i
such that
b^{(k,l)}_{i}
occurs in
\nu
, we have for
i>n
and
1\leq j\leq i
that

\lambda_{2j-1}^{(i+1-j)}=\lambda_{2j}^{(i+1-j)}=0
, and

\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(i+1-j)}\right]^{(k,l)}=0\cdot\left[\lambda_{2j}^{(i+1-j)}\right]^{(l,k)}\in\,_{i+1-j}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}\,\cdot

We now fix
j\geq 1
.

(1)

Suppose now that for some
2\leq i\leq n-j
, (
5.1.1
) holds for
i+1
.

(a)

Assume that
\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,l)}=0\cdot\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(l,k)}\in\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}
.
We first observe that, (
3.1.1
) is equivalent to

s^{(k,l)}_{i-1}\geq\lambda_{2j-1}-s_{0}^{(k,l)}\cdot\lambda_{2j}>s^{(k,l)}_{i-1}-s^{(k,l)}_{i}\,,

and by (
1
), doing (
3.1.2
) consists of replacing
\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,l)}
by
\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)},\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,l)})
, and
\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(l,k)}
by
\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i-1}\mathcal{C}^{(l,k)},\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(l,k)})
.
Otherwise,

s^{(k,l)}_{i-1}-s^{(k,l)}_{i}\geq\lambda_{2j-1}-s_{0}^{(k,l)}\cdot\lambda_{2j}\geq 0\,,

and by (
1
), doing (
3.1.3
) consists of replacing
\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,l)}
by
\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)},\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,l)})
, and
\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(l,k)}
by
\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i-1}\mathcal{C}^{(l,k)},\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(l,k)})
. Therefore, the pair
(\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,l)},\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(l,k)})
is replaced by the pair

(\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)},\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,l)}),\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i-1}\mathcal{C}^{(l,k)},\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(l,k)}))
, and by Lemma
4.15
,

0\cdot\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i-1}\mathcal{C}^{(l,k)},\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(l,k)})=\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)},0\cdot\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(l,k)})=\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)},\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,l)})\,\cdot

As
\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(i+1)}\right]^{(k,l)}=0\cdot\left[\lambda_{2j}^{(i+1)}\right]^{(l,k)}\in\,_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}\subset\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}
, we indeed retrieve fact
(1.a)
and (
5.1.1
) holds for
i
.

(b)

Note that, by (
1
), there are as many stored
b^{(k,l)}_{i-1}
for the insertion into the pair
(\lambda_{2j+1},\lambda_{2j+2})
as following sequences of
\lambda_{2j-1}^{(i+1)}
that belong to
{}_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}
, i.e.

m_{i-1}^{(j+1)}=\sharp\left\{(c_{i})\in\,_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}:\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(i+1)}\right]^{(k,l)}\prec(c_{i})\preceq\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(i)}\right]^{(k,l)}\right\}\,\cdot

Thus,
{}_{i+1}\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(i)}\right]^{(k,l)}=\mathcal{F}(m_{i-1}^{(j+1)},\,_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)},\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(i+1)}\right]^{(k,l)})
, and we retrieve fact
(1.b)
.

(2)

Finally, as
a^{(l,k)}_{0}=0
, the insertion of
b^{(k,l)}_{1}
does not affect the size of
\lambda_{2j}^{(2)}
, and then
\lambda_{2j}^{(1)}=\lambda_{2j}^{(2)}
.

(a)

Since
a^{(k,l)}_{1}=1
,
\lambda_{2j}^{(1)}=\lambda_{2j}^{(2)}+m_{1}^{(j)}
, and fact
(2.a)
holds.

(b)

Reciprocally, fact
(2.b)
follows from (
5.1.1
) for
i=2
and the fact
\lambda_{2j}^{(1)}=\lambda_{2j}^{(2)}
.

B.9.
Proof of Proposition
5.3

Note that for
i\geq 1
,
m_{i}
recursively derives the numbers
m_{i^{\prime}}^{(j)}
for
i^{\prime}
at most equal to
i+1-j
, as the
b^{(k,l)}_{i-j}
inserted into the pair
(\lambda_{2j+1},\lambda_{2j+2})
can only be provided by the
b^{(k,l)}_{i+1-j}
or
b^{(k,l)}_{i+2-j}
inserted into the pair
(\lambda_{2j-1},\lambda_{2j})
.
Let
n
be such that
b^{(k,l)}_{i}
does not occur in
\nu
for
i\geq 2n+1
. For
i\geq 2n+1
and
1\leq j\leq i
, we have
2i-j+1\geq 2n+1
, and then

\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i+1-2j)}=\lambda_{2j}^{(2i+1-2j)}=0
, and

\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i+1-2j)}\right]^{(k,l)}=0\cdot\left[\lambda_{2j}^{(2i+1-2j)}\right]^{(l,k)}\in\,_{i+1-j}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}\,\cdot

We now fix
j\geq 1
.

(1)

Suppose that for some
1\leq i\leq 2n+1-j
, (
5.2.1
) is satisfied for
2i+1
.

(a)

First, observe that, for
i\geq 2
,

\displaystyle\lambda_{2j-1}-s^{(k,1)}_{0}\cdot\lambda_{2j}>s^{(k,1)}_{2i-2}-s^{(k,1)}_{2i}

\displaystyle\Longleftrightarrow s^{(k,1)}_{2}\lambda_{2j-1}-s^{(k,1)}_{0}(\lambda_{2j}-\lambda_{2j-1})>s^{(k,1)}_{2i-2}-s^{(k,1)}_{2i}

\displaystyle\Longleftrightarrow\lambda_{2j-1}-s_{0}^{(k-2)}(\lambda_{2j}-\lambda_{2j-1})>s_{i-1}^{(k-2)}-s_{i}^{(k-2)}\,\cdot

Hence, if
\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,1)}=p_{1}\left(\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)=0\cdot p_{2}\left(\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\in\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}
, then

\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k-2)}=0\cdot\left[\lambda_{2j}-\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k-2)}\in\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k-2)}\,,

By (
1
) and (
1
), doing (
3.1.5
) or (
3.1.6
) (according to whether (
3.1.4
) holds or not) is equivalent to replacing the pair

(\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k-2)},\left[\lambda_{2j}-\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k-2)})

by
\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k-2)},\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k-2)}),\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i-1}\mathcal{C}^{(k-2)},\left[\lambda_{2j}-\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k-2)})
. Hence, by Lemma
4.15
, the new pair also satisfies

\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,1)}=p_{1}\left(\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)=0\cdot p_{2}\left(\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\in\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}\,,

so that (
5.2.1
) recursively holds.

Let us now set
\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}=\mathcal{F}(m_{2i}^{(j)},\,_{+(i-1)}\mathcal{C}^{(1,k)},\left[\lambda_{2j}^{(2i+1)}\right]^{(1,k)})
. By (
4.4.8
),

m_{2i}^{(j)}=a+2b-\chi\left(p_{1}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)=0\cdot\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)},p_{2}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\right)\,,

where

\displaystyle a

\displaystyle=\sharp\left\{r\in\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}\setminus\,_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}:\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i+1)}\right]^{(k,1)}\prec r\preceq p_{1}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\right\}\,,

\displaystyle b

\displaystyle=\sharp\left\{r\in\,_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}:\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i+1)}\right]^{(k,1)}\prec r\preceq p_{1}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\right\}\,\cdot

(i)

On the other hand, using (
1
) and (
1
), at the end of the insertion of
b^{(k,1)}_{2i}
, we have

m_{2i}^{(j)}=a^{\prime}+2b^{\prime}-\chi\left(b^{(k,1)}_{2i-2}\text{ is stored}\right)\,,

where

\displaystyle a^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\sharp\left\{r\in\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}\setminus\,_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}:\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i+1)}\right]^{(k,1)}\prec r\preceq\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i)}\right]^{(k,1)}\right\}\,,

\displaystyle b^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\sharp\left\{r\in\,_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}:\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i+1)}\right]^{(k,1)}\prec r\preceq\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i)}\right]^{(k,1)}\right\}\,\cdot

Assume that
\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i)}\right]^{(k,1)}\succ p_{1}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)
. We then have

\displaystyle a^{\prime}-a

\displaystyle=\sharp\left\{r\in\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}\setminus\,_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}:p_{1}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\prec r\preceq\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i)}\right]^{(k,1)}\right\}\,,

\displaystyle b^{\prime}-b

\displaystyle=\sharp\left\{r\in\,_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}:p_{1}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\prec r\preceq\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i)}\right]^{(k,1)}\right\}\,

\displaystyle a^{\prime}+b^{\prime}-a-b

\displaystyle=\sharp\left\{r\in\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}:p_{1}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\prec r\preceq\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i)}\right]^{(k,1)}\right\}

\displaystyle\geq 1\,\cdot

Hence,

\displaystyle D

\displaystyle=\chi\left(b^{(k,1)}_{2i-2}\text{ is stored}\right)-\chi\left(p_{1}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)=0\cdot\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)},p_{2}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=a^{\prime}-a+2(b^{\prime}-b)

\displaystyle=a^{\prime}+b^{\prime}-a-b+(b^{\prime}-b)\geq 1

and this is only possible when the equality holds, as
\chi(prop)\in\{0,1\}
. Then,
b^{(k,1)}_{2i-2}
is stored and
p_{1}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\prec 0\cdot\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)},p_{2}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)
. However,
b^{(k,1)}_{2i-2}
can only be stored at the end of the insertion of the
b^{(k,1)}_{2i}
with an occurrence of (
3.1.6
), and by (
1
), this means that
\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i)}\right]^{(k,1)}\in\,_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}
. Thus,
b^{\prime}-b\geq 1
, and
D\geq 2
, and a contradiction occurs. With the same reasonning, we show that we cannot have
\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i)}\right]^{(k,1)}\prec p_{1}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)
. Therefore,
\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i)}\right]^{(k,1)}=p_{1}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)
and we obtain fact
(1.a.ii)
. Remark that
a=a^{\prime}
,
b=b^{\prime}
and

b^{(k,1)}_{2i-2}
is stored if and only if
p_{1}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)=0\cdot\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)},p_{2}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)
.

(ii)

Fact
(1.a.ii)
is straightforward, since we insert once
b^{(k,1)}_{2i-1}
whenever (
3.1.4
) occurs or not.

(b)

Reciprocally, let us consider
\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i-1)}\right]^{(1,k)}=\left(p_{1}\left(\left[\lambda_{2j}^{(2i-1)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right),\,_{i}p_{2}\left(\left[\lambda_{2j}^{(2i-1)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\right)\,\cdot

By (
5.2.1
) in
2i-1
,

0\cdot p_{2}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)=\,_{i+1}p_{1}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\preceq\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i-1)}\right]^{(k,1)}\prec 0\cdot\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)},p_{2}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\,\cdot

Thus, by (
4.4.8
), if
\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i-1)}\right]^{(1,k)}=\mathcal{F}(m,\,_{+(i-1)}\mathcal{C}^{(1,k)},\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)})
, then

m=a+2b+\chi\left(p_{1}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)=0\cdot\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)},p_{2}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\right)

where

\displaystyle a

\displaystyle=\sharp\left\{r\in\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}\setminus\,_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}:\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i)}\right]^{(k,1)}\prec r\preceq\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i-1)}\right]^{(k,1)}\right\}\,,

\displaystyle b

\displaystyle=\sharp\left\{r\in\,_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}:\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i)}\right]^{(k,1)}\prec r\preceq\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i-1)}\right]^{(k,1)}\right\}\,\cdot

(i)

Since for each inserted
b^{(k,1)}_{2i-1}
, we store one
b^{(k,1)}_{2i-2}
when we do (
3.1.6
) and two
b^{(k,1)}_{2i-2}
when we do (
3.1.5
), the quantity
a+2b
then represents the numbers of stored
b^{(k,1)}_{2i-2}
during the insertions of
b^{(k,1)}_{2i-1}
. In addition,
p_{1}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)=0\cdot\mathcal{F}\left(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)},p_{2}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\right)
if and only if one
b^{(k,1)}_{2i-2}
is stored at the end of the insertions of
b^{(k,1)}_{2i-1}
. Therefore,
m=m_{2j-2}^{(j+1)}
and we retrieve fact
(2.b.i)
.

(ii)

Recall that, in
(1.a.i)
,

\displaystyle Q

\displaystyle=\sharp\left\{r\in\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}:p_{2}\left(\left[\lambda_{2j}^{(2i+1)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\prec r\preceq p_{2}\left(\left[\mu_{2j}^{(2i)}\right]^{(1,k)}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=\sharp\left\{r\in\,_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)}:\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i+1)}\right]^{(k,1)}\prec r\preceq\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2i)}\right]^{(k,1)}\right\}-\chi\left(b^{(k,1)}_{2i-2}\text{ is stored}\right)\,\cdot

But this quantity is exactly the number of
b^{(k,1)}_{2i-1}
stored during the insertions of
b^{(k,1)}_{2i}
. Hence
Q=m_{2i-1}^{(j+1)}
.

(2)

Remark that applying (
3.1.8
) is equivalent to replacing
(\lambda_{2j-1},\lambda_{2j}-\lambda_{2j-1})
by

(\lambda_{2j-1},\lambda_{2j}-\lambda_{2j-1}+1)
, and applying (
3.1.6
) for
i=2
is equivalent to replacing

(\lambda_{2j-1},\lambda_{2j}-\lambda_{2j-1})
by
(\lambda_{2j-1}+1,\lambda_{2j}-\lambda_{2j-1})
. Moreover,

\displaystyle\lambda_{2j-1}-s^{(k,1)}_{0}\cdot\lambda_{2j}>s^{(k,1)}_{0}

\displaystyle\Longleftrightarrow s^{(k,1)}_{2}\lambda_{2j-1}-s^{(k,1)}_{0}(\lambda_{2j}-\lambda_{2j-1})>s^{(k,1)}_{0}

Hence, if
(\lambda_{2j-1},\lambda_{2j}-\lambda_{2j-1})=(0,0)
, we replace by
(1,0)
, so that by fact
(1.a)
of Lemma
4.24
,
[\lambda_{2j}]^{(1,k)}=(\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,1)},[\lambda_{2j}-\lambda_{2j-1})]^{(k,1)})
is replaced by
\mathcal{F}(1,\mathcal{C}^{(1,k)},[\lambda_{2j}]^{(1,k)})
, and
\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,1)}
is replaced by
p_{1}(\mathcal{F}(1,\mathcal{C}^{(1,k)},[\lambda_{2j}]^{(1,k)}))
. Otherwise, assume that
\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,1)}=p_{1}([\lambda_{2j}]^{(1,k)})
. Then, (
3.1.7
) is equivalent to

1>s^{(k,1)}_{2}\lambda_{2j-1}-s^{(k,1)}_{0}(\lambda_{2j}-\lambda_{2j-1})>s^{(k,1)}_{0}

so that by (
2.2.2
) and (
4.4.1
),
p_{1}([\lambda_{2j}]^{(1,k)})=0\cdot\mathcal{F}(1,\mathcal{C}^{(k,1)},p_{2}([\lambda_{2j}]^{(1,k)}))
. Therefore, by fact
(1.b)
of Lemma
4.24
in
d=0
,

\mathcal{F}(1,\mathcal{C}^{(1,k)},[\lambda_{2j}]^{(1,k)})=([\lambda_{2j-1}]^{(k,1)},[\lambda_{2j}-\lambda_{2j-1}+1]^{(1,k)})\,\cdot

Similarly, when (
3.1.7
) does not occur, by fact
(1.a)
of Lemma
4.24
in
d=0
,

\mathcal{F}(1,\mathcal{C}^{(1,k)},[\lambda_{2j}]^{(1,k)})=([\lambda_{2j-1}+1]^{(k,1)},[\lambda_{2j}-\lambda_{2j-1}]^{(1,k)})\,\cdot

We thus retrieve the fact that
[\lambda_{2j}]^{(1,k)}=(\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,1)},[\lambda_{2j}-\lambda_{2j-1})]^{(k,1)})
is replaced by

\mathcal{F}(1,\mathcal{C}^{(1,k)},[\lambda_{2j}]^{(1,k)})
, and
\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,1)}
is replaced by
p_{1}(\mathcal{F}(1,\mathcal{C}^{(1,k)},[\lambda_{2j}]^{(1,k)}))
.
Therefore, fact
(2.a)
holds. Moreover, we store as many
b^{(k,1)}_{1}
as occurrences of fact
(1.b)
of Lemma
4.24
in
d=0
, and fact
(2.b)
follows.

(3)

Fact
(3)
is straightforward as its proof follows the same reasoning as the proof of fact

of Proposition

.

B.11.
Proof of Proposition
6.1

Set
m_{2n+1}=0
the number of inserted
b^{(k,l)}_{2n+1}
in
\lambda_{2n},\lambda_{2n-1}
. Note that for
2n+1\geq i\geq 1
,
m_{i}
recursively derives the numbers
m_{i-j}^{(n-j)}
and the pairs
(\lambda_{2n-2j}^{(i-j)},\lambda_{2n-2j-1}^{(i-j)})
for
1\leq j\leq\max\{n-1,i-1\}
, as the
b^{(k,l)}_{i-j}
inserted into the pair
(\lambda_{2n-2j},\lambda_{2n-2j-1})
can only be provided by the
b^{(k,l)}_{i+1-j}
inserted into the pair
(\lambda_{2n-2j+2},\lambda_{2n-2j+1})
.
We thus have for
1\leq j\leq n
that

\lambda_{2j}^{(n+1+j)}=\lambda_{2j-1}^{(n+1+j)}=0
.

(1)

Suppose that for some
n\geq j>1
,
m_{i}^{(j)}=0
and
\lambda_{2j}^{(i)}=\lambda_{2j-1}^{(i)}=0
, for
n+1+j\geq i>2j
. This implies that for
m_{i}^{(j-1)}=0
and
\lambda_{2j-2}^{(i)}=\lambda_{2j-3}^{(i)}=0
, for
n+j\geq i>2j-1
. Moreover, by (
2
), the insertion of
b^{(k,l)}_{2j}
into the pair
(\lambda_{2j},\lambda_{2j-1})
can only be done using (
3.2.3
), so that there is no part
b^{(k,l)}_{2j-1}
stored for the insertion into the pair
(\lambda_{2j-2},\lambda_{2j-3})
. Therefore,
m_{2j-1}^{j-1}=0
,
\lambda_{2j-2}^{(2j-1)}=\lambda_{2j-3}^{(2j-1)}=0
, and we conclude that
m_{i}^{(j-1)}=0
and
\lambda_{2j-2}^{(i)}=\lambda_{2j-3}^{(i)}=0
, for
n+j\geq i>2j-2
. Since the assumption holds for
j=n
, fact
(1)
recursively holds for
n\geq j\geq 1
.

(2)

As the insertion of
b^{(k,l)}_{2j}
into the pair
(\lambda_{2j},\lambda_{2j-1})
only consists of iteration of (
3.2.3
), and
\lambda_{2j}^{(2j+1)}=\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2j+1)}=0
, we then obtain that

(\lambda_{2j}^{(2j)},\lambda_{2j-1}^{(2j)})=(m_{2j}^{(j)}a^{(k,l)}_{2j},m_{2j}^{(j)}a^{(l,k)}_{2j-1})
. Hence, the relation (
6.1.1
) holds for
i=2j
, as well as fact
(2.a)
.

Assume that
\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(k,l)}_{2j}=0\cdot\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(l,k)}_{2j-1}\in\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}_{2j}
.
We first observe that, (
3.1.1
) is equivalent to

a^{(k,l)}_{2j+1-i}>a^{(l,k)}_{2j-1}\lambda_{2j}-a^{(k,l)}_{2j}\lambda_{2j-1}\geq a^{(k,l)}_{2j+1-i}-a^{(k,l)}_{2j-i}\,,

and by (
2
), doing (
3.2.2
) consists of replacing
\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(k,l)}_{2j}
by
\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}_{2j},\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,l)}_{2j})
, and
\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(l,k)}_{2j-1}
by
\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i-1}\mathcal{C}^{(l,k)}_{2j-1},\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(l,k)}_{2j-1})
.
Otherwise,

a^{(k,l)}_{2j+1-i}-a^{(k,l)}_{2j-i}>a^{(l,k)}_{2j-1}\lambda_{2j}-a^{(k,l)}_{2j}\lambda_{2j-1}\geq 0\,,

and by (
2
), doing (
3.2.3
) consists of replacing
\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(k,l)}_{2j}
by
\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}_{2j},\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(k,l)}_{2j})
, and
\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(l,k)}_{2j-1}
by
\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i-1}\mathcal{C}^{(l,k)}_{2j-1},\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(l,k)}_{2j-1})
. Therefore, the pair
(\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(k,l)}_{2j},\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(l,k)}_{2j-1})
is replaced by the pair

(\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}_{2j},\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(k,l)}_{2j}),\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i-1}\mathcal{C}^{(l,k)}_{2j-1},\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(l,k)}_{2j-1}))
, and by Lemma
4.15
,

0\cdot\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i-1}\mathcal{C}^{(l,k)}_{2j-1},\left[\lambda_{2j-1}\right]^{(l,k)}_{2j-1})=\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}_{2j},0\cdot\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(l,k)}_{2j-1})=\mathcal{F}(1,\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}_{2j},\left[\lambda_{2j}\right]^{(k,l)}_{2j})\,\cdot

As
\left[\lambda_{2j}^{(i+1)}\right]^{(k,l)}_{2j}=0\cdot\left[\lambda_{2j-1}^{(i+1)}\right]^{(l,k)}_{2j-1}\in\,_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}_{2j}\subset\,_{i}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}_{2j}
, we indeed retrieve fact
(2.a)
and (
6.1.1
) holds for
i
.

(3)

Note that, by (
2
), there are as many stored
b^{(k,l)}_{i-1}
for the insertion into the pair
(\lambda_{2j-2},\lambda_{2j-3})
as following sequences of
[\lambda_{2j}^{(i+1)}]^{(k,l)}_{2j}
that belong to
{}_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}_{2j}
, i.e.

m_{i-1}^{(j-1)}=\sharp\left\{r\in\,_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}_{2j}:\left[\lambda_{2j}^{(i+1)}\right]^{(k,l)}_{2j}\prec r\preceq\left[\lambda_{2j}^{(i)}\right]^{(k,l)}_{2j}\right\}\,\cdot

Thus,
{}_{i+1}\left[\lambda_{2j}^{(i)}\right]^{(k,l)}_{2j}=\mathcal{F}(m_{i-1}^{(j-1)},\,_{i+1}\mathcal{C}^{(k,l)}_{2j},\left[\lambda_{2j}^{(i+1)}\right]^{(k,l)}_{2j})
, and we retrieve fact
(2.b)
.

(4)

Finally, as
a^{(l,k)}_{0}=0
, and
a^{(k,l)}_{1}=1
, fact
(3)
follows.
